- **learnings**: Capture a learning that outlives the current repository into the author's personal knowledge repo, or recall what earlier sessions learned about a topic. - **leaspec-archive**: Archive a completed change by merging spec deltas into the main specs and moving the change to the archive. Use after leaspec-review is APPROVED. - **leaspec-execute**: Execute implementation tasks from an approved plan using adaptive orchestration. Use after leaspec-plan when tasks.md is ready.
